Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAOAM，一个统一的对象与材质选择框架，支持文本和点击交互，通过VLM+分割头实现像素级精确掩码，并利用多任务训练和可扩展数据生成管线，在多种编辑场景中表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的选择方法以对象为中心且仅支持单一交互模态，缺乏材质级别的选择能力，限制了实际图像编辑中的适用性。

Method: 1）采用VLM+分割头的架构，VLM理解用户意图并编码视觉实体与空间关系，分割头解码为掩码；2）提出可扩展的数据生成管线，利用真实/合成图像+材质掩码，结合VLM生成富语义的材质描述；3）多任务训练：文本/点击选择任务+辅助VQA任务促进材质理解；4）推理时支持文本与点击模态组合。

Result: 实验表明MAOAM在对象、材质及多种交互场景下均实现了准确且连贯的选择，并展现出鲁棒性；尽管仅使用单模态提示训练，推理时联合文本与点击可带来选择性能的涌现提升。

Conclusion: MAOAM统一了对象与材质级别的选择，支持文本和点击双模态交互，为交互式图像编辑提供了更灵活、精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Selection is a core operation in interactive image editing. To be practical, a user should be able to specify and disambiguate the desired selection region through either text or click-based interactions, and the system should support selecting not only objects but also other criteria, such as materials. Material-based selection is valuable for tasks like re-texturing surfaces or editing instances of a specific material. However, existing vision-language-model (VLM) based selection methods are object-centric and typically support a single interaction modality, limiting their applicability. In this work, we thus present Mask Any Object And Material (MAOAM), a unified selection framework that enables precise object and material-level selection across both text- and click-based interactions. MAOAM leverages a VLM with a segmentation head to produce pixel-accurate masks from user prompts: the VLM interprets the user's selection intent (object or material-level) and encodes visual entities, attributes, and spatial relations, while the segmentation head decodes the output token into a mask. A key challenge is the lack of material selection datasets with text annotations. We propose a scalable data generation pipeline: we collect real and synthetic images with material masks, and leverage VLMs to generate material descriptions with rich visual-semantics. We train MAOAM with a multi-task objective over click and text-based selection, along with an auxiliary VQA task derived from the material descriptions to facilitate deeper material understanding. Despite being trained with uni-modal prompts, our model exhibits an emergent improvement in selection when combining text and clicks at inference, enabling flexible image editing workflows. Experiments demonstrate accurate and coherent selections across diverse objects, materials, and interaction scenarios, highlighting robustness in practice.

</details>


### [76] [DiverAge: Reliable Pluralistic Face Aging with Cross-Age Identity Relation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04881)
*Yueying Zou,Peipei Li,Qianrui Teng,Dianyan Xu,Zekun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiverAge框架，基于扩散自编码实现分层多元人脸老化，通过CARR机制在推理时引导多年龄组联合去噪，在保持外观多样性的同时提升序列级身份可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸老化方法要么缺乏随机多样性（确定性方法），要么未能明确调控完整老化序列的身份演化（多元化方法），无法在保证外观多样性的同时维护跨年龄身份演化的序列级序数可靠性。

Method: 提出DiverAge框架：基于扩散自编码（扩散解码+年龄条件语义调制）实现外观级多样性；提出CARR（跨年龄身份关系调节器），利用真实跨年龄同身份对的CIS先验，在推理时通过单侧采样时间引导联合去噪多目标年龄组，无需修改训练目标或引入额外参数。

Result: DiverAge在保持身份保留、年龄准确度、图像质量和外观级多样性的同时，改善了序列级序数可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过扩散自编码和推理时CARR引导策略，实现了外观多样性与跨年龄身份演化可靠性的兼得。

Abstract: Face aging plays an important role in long-term biometric analysis, cross-age identity verification, and forensic identity analysis. Since the same subject may exhibit multiple plausible appearances at a target age due to genetic, environmental, and lifestyle factors, face aging is inherently a one-to-many generation problem. However, pluralism alone is insufficient for reliable face aging: a model should provide appearance-level candidate diversity within each age group while maintaining sequence-level ordinal reliability across ordered age groups. Existing deterministic aging methods can synthesize visually plausible age-progressed faces, but usually lack stochastic diversity. In contrast, pluralistic aging methods introduce local appearance variations, but often fail to explicitly regulate the identity evolution of the full aging sequence. In this paper, we propose \textbf{DiverAge}, a hierarchical pluralistic face aging framework based on diffusion autoencoding. DiverAge preserves appearance-level diversity through stochastic diffusion decoding and age-conditioned semantic modulation. To improve sequence-level reliability, we introduce a Cross-age Identity Relation Regulator (CARR), an inference-time guidance strategy that jointly denoises multiple target age groups. CARR is guided by a Cross-age Identity Similarity (CIS) prior estimated from real same-identity cross-age pairs, and suppresses excessive cross-age identity drift through one-sided sampling-time guidance without modifying the training objective or introducing extra trainable parameters. Experiments demonstrate that DiverAge improves sequence-level ordinal reliability while maintaining identity preservation, age accuracy, image quality, and appearance-level diversity.

</details>


### [77] [HD-DinoMoE: A Class-Aware Hierarchical Dual Mixture-of-Experts Network for Scleral Anomaly Segmentation in Complex Acquisition Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04888)
*Yinxiang Yu,Maoxiang Chu,Qi Niu,Guanghu Liu,Wei Xu,Haotian Wang,Zhi Chen,Yutian Zhu,Yuelong Fan,Guanghao Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HD-DinoMoE网络，用于中医目诊中巩膜表面异常（血管、黄黑斑、血斑）的像素级分割，在ML-SASD数据集上达到72.11%的平均Dice系数，有效解决了多源分布差异、异常形态多样和巩膜镜面反射等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中医目诊中巩膜表面异常评估依赖主观经验、难以量化，现有图像分割方法受多源分布差异、异常形态多样及巩膜镜面反射（SSR）等因素影响，缺乏有效的自动化辅助诊断方案。

Method: 提出HD-DinoMoE网络：1）类感知双流DINOv3特征融合；2）类特定多专家解码；3）三阶段骨干冻结路由策略稳定双骨干适配；4）渐进置信惩罚（PCP）损失降低SSR区域假阳性；5）类感知自适应样本加权（CA-ASW）平衡训练贡献。同时构建多标签巩膜异常分割数据集（ML-SASD）。

Result: 在ML-SASD-Mix上，平均Dice为72.11%，平均IoU为58.44%；在边界定位和镜面区域假阳性控制方面表现良好；在公开SBVPI数据集的血管子集上具有竞争力的泛化性能。

Conclusion: HD-DinoMoE为复杂采集场景下的中医目诊辅助诊断（TAO）提供了可行的分割解决方案，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) ocular inspection provides empirical cues for assessing scleral surface anomalies, but its clinical use remains subjective and difficult to quantify. To support intelligent and quantifiable ocular inspection, this study presents the TCM-inspired Artificial Intelligence Ocular Auxiliary Diagnosis System (TAO) and focuses on pixel-level scleral surface anomaly segmentation. For clinical and user-acquired images affected by multi-source distributional discrepancies, diverse anomaly morphologies, and scleral specular reflection (SSR), we propose HD-DinoMoE, a class-aware hierarchical dual mixture-of-experts network. HD-DinoMoE combines class-aware dual-stream DINOv3 feature fusion with class-specific multi-expert decoding to segment Vessels, Yellow and Black Spots, and Blood Spots. A three-stage backbone-frozen routing strategy stabilizes dual-backbone adaptation; Progressive Confidence Penalty (PCP) Loss reduces high-confidence false positives and segmentation leakage in SSR regions; and Class-Aware Adaptive Sample Weighting (CA-ASW) balances sample- and class-level training contributions. We further construct the Multi-label Scleral Anomaly Segmentation Dataset (ML-SASD), a new benchmark with Clinical, Wild, and Mix settings and pixel-wise annotations for three anomaly categories. On ML-SASD-Mix, HD-DinoMoE achieves a mean Dice of 72.11% and a mean Intersection-over-Union of 58.44%, while maintaining favorable boundary localization and specular-region false-positive control. It also shows competitive generalization on the Vessels subset of the public SBVPI dataset. These results indicate that HD-DinoMoE provides a feasible segmentation solution for TAO under complex acquisition scenarios. The code and data access information are available at https://github.com/FX-CMX/HD-DinoMoE.

</details>


### [78] [Hierarchical Space Partition for Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04891)
*Minjie Tang,Xiangfei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于平面组装策略的点云多边形模型重建方法，通过将平面分为三类（高可见、低可见、不可见）并采用优先级增长和层次化空间划分，结合最小割优化生成水密多边形网格，有效恢复LiDAR扫描中缺失的细节信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR扫描存在距离限制和遮挡等固有缺陷，导致关键场景信息缺失，传统方法重建精度下降。因此需要一种能有效恢复缺失细节并保持模型紧凑性的方法。

Method: 将场景中提取的平面分为高可见、低可见和不可见三类（不可见平面通过场景结构分析恢复）；按优先级层次化划分空间；通过最小割优化生成水密多边形网格。

Result: 在公开数据集上对比主流方法，验证了该方法在重建精度和效果上的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的平面组装与层次化划分策略能够有效恢复LiDAR点云中缺失的场景结构，生成紧凑且水密的多边形模型，优于现有主流方法。

Abstract: Generating compact polygonal models from point clouds is a key problem in 3D vision and computer graphics. However, due to inherent limitations of LiDAR scanning (e.g. range constraints and occlusions), critical scene information is often missing, leading to degraded reconstruction accuracy. To address this, we propose a plane assembling strategy that effectively recovers missing details while maintaining model compactness. We classify all the planes extracted from the scene into three categories: highly visible, barely visible, and invisible. The invisible planes, which are recovered by scene structure analysis, indicate the missing details. The three types of planes correspond to the three growth priorities. Each plane grows according to the priority level, and the space is partitioned progressively, namely, the hierarchical partition. Subsequently, we generate a watertight polygonal mesh from the partition via a min-cut-based optimization. Finally, comparisons on public datasets show the effectiveness and superiority of our method against mainstream approaches. The project page is available at https://hsr-3dv.github.io/.

</details>


### [79] [CDPM-Align: Multi-Scale Guidance-Aligned Diffusion Pretraining for Robust Few-Shot Anatomical Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04898)
*Roberto Di Via,Irina Voiculescu,Francesca Odone,Vito Paolo Pastore*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CDPM-align是一种多尺度引导对齐的条件扩散预训练方法，用于解剖标志点检测。在少量图像和少量标注（10或25张）的条件下，该方法通过学习鲁棒表征，提升了检测精度和不确定性估计，推动了安全高效的临床部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有方法在解剖标志点定位上已达到亚毫米级精度，但单靠精度不足以满足临床部署对可靠性和鲁棒性的要求。目前表征学习在这一场景下的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CDPM-align，一种多尺度引导对齐的条件扩散预训练方法。在三个异质性小规模基准数据集上进行条件生成式预训练，然后在低标注（10或25张图像）的下游任务中进行微调，用于解剖标志点检测。

Result: 生成式预训练使模型学习到鲁棒的表征，在低标注场景下同时提升了标志点检测的精度和不确定性估计性能。

Conclusion: CDPM-align验证了条件扩散生成式预训练在小样本和低标注场景下的有效性，为安全高效的临床部署提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Anatomical landmark detection is a fundamental task in medical image analysis supporting a wide range of diagnostic and interventional workflows. Although recent methods have achieved sub-millimetric localisation, accuracy alone is not sufficient for clinical deployment, requiring reliability and robustness in prediction. Despite its clinical relevance, the impact of representation learning in this context is still underexplored. In this work, we introduce CDPM-align, a multi-scale guidance-aligned conditional diffusion pre-training for anatomical landmark detection. Our experimental setup focuses on a few images and a few annotation regimes. Specifically, we employ three popular heterogeneous small-scale benchmark datasets for representation learning via conditional generative pre-training. Furthermore, we consider low-annotation scenarios for the downstream task of landmark detection, with 10 and 25 annotated images, reflecting realistic trade-offs between clinical effort and resource constraints for annotations. Our results confirm that generative pre-training enables the model to learn a robust representation. This improves both accuracy and uncertainty on the downstream tasks, advancing towards safe and efficient clinical deployment.

</details>


### [80] [BreastGPT: A Multimodal Large Language Model for the Full Spectrum of Breast Cancer Clinical Routine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04911)
*Yang Liu,Jiajin Zhang,Danyang Tu,Yaojun Hu,Jiao Qu,Jiuyu Zhang,Yu Shi,Wei Fang,Shi Gu,Ling Zhang,Yingda Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了BreastStage（含186万指令对的乳腺癌影像工作流数据集）和BreastGPT（双分支视觉编码器+概念保持令牌压缩的统一多模态大模型），在乳腺癌筛查、诊断和治疗规划的全工作流推理中优于现有通用和医学专用MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态大模型受限于数据稀缺和模型通用性，只能在孤立模态或狭窄任务上评估，无法支持乳腺癌临床管理（筛查-诊断-治疗规划）中跨模态、多阶段的工作流级临床推理。

Method: 构建BreastStage数据集（17个子数据集、5种影像模态、136个任务模板、186万指令对），并设计BreastGPT模型，采用双分支视觉编码器处理标准放射影像和千兆像素病理图像，结合概念保持令牌压缩技术弥合尺度差异。

Result: 在BreastStage-Bench上，BreastGPT闭式准确率75.66%，开式评分89.92%，在临床阶段和任务格式上均优于通用和医学专用MLLM。

Conclusion: 工作流对齐的数据和跨尺度视觉建模对于构建临床落地的医学多模态大模型至关重要。

Abstract: Breast cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality among women. Its clinical management requires multimodal reasoning across a clinical workflow that spans \textit{screening}, \textit{diagnosis} and \textit{treatment planning}, where each stage involves distinct imaging modalities, task objectives, and reasoning patterns. However, constrained by data scarcity and model versatility, existing medical MLLMs are typically evaluated on isolated modalities or narrow task families, limiting their ability to support workflow-level clinical reasoning. In this work, we first introduce \textbf{BreastStage}, a workflow-aligned breast imaging instruction corpus comprising 1.86M instruction-following pairs curated from 17 sub-datasets across 5 imaging modalities and 136 task templates. Its held-out split, \textbf{BreastStage-Bench}, provides a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating multimodal reasoning across the breast cancer care continuum. Building on this corpus, we propose \textbf{BreastGPT}, a unified MLLM equipped with a dual-branch visual encoder and concept-preserving token compression to bridge the scale gap between standard radiology and gigapixel pathology. On BreastStage-Bench, BreastGPT achieves 75.66\% closed-ended accuracy and 89.92\% open-ended score, outperforming both general-purpose and medical-specific MLLMs across clinical stages and task formats. These results suggest that workflow-aligned data and cross-scale visual modeling are critical for clinically grounded medical MLLMs. All data, code, and model checkpoints are released at https://yangyy-liu.github.io/BreastGPT.io.

</details>


### [81] [Geometry-Aware Distillation for Prompt Tuning Biomedical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04922)
*Tran Dinh Tien,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Omni-Geometry知识蒸馏框架（OGKD），通过注入类别关系结构到教师模型中，生成保持真实标签同时尊重类间几何关系的方向性目标，并设计两种蒸馏损失（全局GAD和局部LGD），在11个医学数据集上平均提升1.7%-2.8%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调方法仅优化真实标签类别，忽略临床上有意义的类间关系，导致在有限标注场景下决策边界不稳定。

Method: OGKD框架：1) 将类别关系结构注入教师模型生成方向性目标；2) 全局几何感知蒸馏（GAD）作用于全局图像token；3) 标签引导几何蒸馏（LGD）作用于注意力补丁token以提升细粒度对齐。

Result: 在11个医学数据集上，base-to-novel和少样本评估中平均绝对增益1.7%-2.8%，优于所有现有SOTA VLM适配方法，且对未见类别有良好泛化性。

Conclusion: OGKD能有效利用类间几何关系，在保护隐私的冻结骨干网络中显著提升医学图像分类性能，泛化性和可靠性优于现有方法。

Abstract: Current prompt-based and adapter-based tuning of vision-language models (VLMs) is attractive for medical imaging, where clinical data sensitivity favors frozen backbones and annotations are limited. However, these methods typically optimize only the ground-truth class, treating all other classes as equally incorrect, ignoring clinically meaningful class relations and yielding unstable decision boundaries in limited-supervision settings. We propose Omni-Geometry Knowledge Distillation (OGKD), a new framework that injects class-relation structure into the teacher to produce directional targets that preserve the ground truth while respecting inter-class geometry. Using these targets, we develop two distillation losses: Global Geometry-Aware Distillation (GAD) operates on the global image token, and Label-Guided Geometry Distillation (LGD) applies the same geometry to attentive patch tokens to improve fine-grained alignment. Across comprehensive experiments and analyses on 11 widely-used medical datasets for base-to-novel and few-shot evaluations, our OGKD achieves substantially better performance, consistently improving accuracy by an average absolute gain of 1.7%-2.8% over all prior state-of-the-art VLM adaptation counterparts. It also robustly generalizes to unseen classes and yields more reliable predictions than other approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/tientrandinh/OGKD.

</details>


### [82] [Scene-Centric Unsupervised Video Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04925)
*Christoph Reich,Oliver Hahn,Nikita Araslanov,Laura Leal-Taixé,Christian Rupprecht,Daniel Cremers,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个无监督视频全景分割方法VideoCUPS，利用深度、运动和视觉线索生成时序一致的伪标签，并引入评估协议和基线，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督场景理解主要聚焦于图像分割任务，视频领域的无监督全景分割尚未被探索。

Method: VideoCUPS利用无监督深度、运动和视觉线索从场景中心视频中生成时序一致的全景视频伪标签，并提出Video DropLoss损失函数进行训练。

Result: VideoCUPS在所有基线上表现最优，并展现出强大的标签高效学习能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过提出方法、评估协议和基线，为未来无监督VPS研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Video panoptic segmentation (VPS) aims to jointly detect, segment, and track all objects while partitioning the video into semantically consistent regions. We introduce the task setting of unsupervised VPS, omitting any human supervision. Existing unsupervised scene understanding works mainly focused on image segmentation tasks; the video domain remains underexplored. We propose VideoCUPS, the first unsupervised VPS approach. VideoCUPS generates temporally consistent panoptic video pseudo-labels from scene-centric videos by exploiting unsupervised depth, motion, and visual cues. Training on these pseudo-labels using a novel Video DropLoss yields an accurate, unsupervised VPS model. To benchmark progress, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation protocol and four competitive baselines, extending state-of-the-art unsupervised panoptic image and instance video segmentation models to VPS. VideoCUPS outperforms all baselines and demonstrates strong label-efficient learning. With VideoCUPS, our evaluation protocol, and baselines, we provide a strong foundation for future research on unsupervised VPS.

</details>


### [83] [Plan, Watch, Recover: A Benchmark and Architectures for Proactive Procedural Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04970)
*Kaustav Kundu,Ritvik Shrivastava,Maxim Arap,Nanshu Wang,Xianhui Zhu,Quintin Fettes,Gautam Tiwari,Parth Suresh,Théo Moutakanni,Alejandro Castillejo Munoz,Allen Bolourchi,Pascale Fung,Pinar Donmez,Babak Damavandi,Anuj Kumar,Seungwhan Moon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoProactive数据集和Pro²Bench基准，并设计了解耦规划器-交互架构，用于构建主动式多模态助手系统，在用户偏离预设步骤时提供实时指导。实验表明，该系统在多个数据集上优于Claude、Gemini、GPT等强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式多模态助手系统缺乏大规模、跨领域的基准数据集，尤其是缺少用户偏离预期步骤（Out-of-Plan）场景的数据支持，限制了该领域的研究进展。

Method: (1) 构建EgoProactive数据集，包含穿戴式第一人称视角的主动过程指导数据及显式的偏离计划(OOP)标注和恢复步骤；(2) 将五个已有基准统一为Pro²Bench；(3) 提出解耦规划器-交互架构，专门处理过程状态、视觉线索和恢复注入；(4) 提出可跨模型家族迁移的后训练方法。

Result: 训练的Llama-4系统在六个数据集上显著优于Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT 5.2等强闭源基线以及Qwen3-VL 235B等开源基线。Oracle计划实验表明，当计划质量可控时，该双工模型能产生高质量指导并在OOP恢复上取得大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为主动式过程辅助系统提供了大规模基准和有效架构，证明了在用户偏离计划场景下实现高质量实时指导的可行性。

Abstract: We envision a proactive multi-modal assistant system which gives users real-time step-by-step guidance on a procedural task, autonomously deciding \textit{when} to interrupt, and \textit{how} to coach. However, progress is limited by the absence of large-scale, cross-domain benchmarks that reflect realistic conditions, particularly the common case in which users deviate from the expected step sequence. We address this gap with four contributions: \textbf{(1)}~we release \textbf{EgoProactive}, a large-scale wearable-egocentric dataset for proactive procedural assistance with explicit Out-of-Plan (OOP) annotations and recovery steps; \textbf{(2)}~we augment five established benchmarks (Ego4D, EPIC-KITCHENS, EgoExo4D, HoloAssist, HowTo100M) into \textbf{Pro\textsuperscript{2}Bench} under a unified proactive-guidance schema; \textbf{(3)}~we propose a \textbf{decoupled planner--interaction architecture} specialized for procedural state, visual cues, and recovery injection; \textbf{(4)}~we introduce a post-training recipe that transfers across model families, validated by cross-backbone replication on Llama~4 and Qwen-3.6-VL. In extensive experiments, our trained Llama-4 system substantially improves objective intervention quality over strong proprietary baselines (Claude Opus~4.6, Gemini~3.1~Pro, GPT~5.2) and open-weight baselines (Qwen3~VL~235B) baselines across all six datasets. Oracle-plan experiments further show that, when plan quality is controlled, the trained duplex model produces high-quality guidance and large gains on Out-of-Plan recovery.

</details>


### [84] [Food-R1: A Unified Multi-Task Food Vision-Language Model with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04986)
*Yu Zhu,Yongkang Li,Wenjie Zhu,Haoyi Jiang,Wenyu Liu,Wei Yang,Bin Li,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CalorieBench-80K基准数据集和Food-R1模型，通过引入思维链(CoT)标注和强化微调(RFT)技术，显著提升了视觉语言模型在食物分析任务中的推理和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物分析VLM方法主要依赖监督微调(SFT)，限制了推理和泛化能力；同时高质量的大规模营养标注数据稀缺。

Method: 构建CalorieBench-80K基准数据集（含CoT标注）；提出Food-R1统一食物VLM，采用多任务学习范式，先进行CoT冷启动指令微调，再使用GRPO进行强化微调(RFT)。

Result: 在CalorieBench-80K及代表性基准测试上，Food-R1在食物相关任务上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合CoT推理和强化微调能有效提升食物VLM的推理能力和性能，为食物分析领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent studies have explored Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for food analysis. However, most existing methods rely primarily on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which often limits reasoning and generalization capabilities. Moreover, high-quality large-scale nutritional annotations remain scarce. To address these issues, we introduce CalorieBench-80K, a large-scale benchmark with curated calorie labels and dietary advice annotations. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first food image benchmark to incorporate Chain-of-Thought (CoT) annotations for calorie reasoning. We also propose Food-R1, a unified food VLM trained in a multi-task learning paradigm to equip the model with broad capabilities. Food-R1 undergoes CoT-based cold-start instruction tuning, followed by reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to improve reasoning and performance. Experiments on CalorieBench-80K and representative benchmarks show that Food-R1 consistently outperforms strong baselines across food-related tasks. The code, model weights, and benchmark annotations are available at the project repository.

</details>


### [85] [Multi-Camera AR Guidance System for Surgical Instrument Handling and Assembly: Investigating Workload and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04992)
*Shiyu Li,Julian Kreimeier,Hannah Schieber,Dirk Müller,Bernhard Kainz,Rüdiger von Eisenhart-Rothe,Daniel Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多相机6D位姿估计与增强现实（AR）头显的器械引导系统，无需额外标记即可辅助手术室护士完成陌生器械的组装。系统仅使用合成数据训练，结合眼动和脚踏板实现步骤切换。技术评估优于现有方法，并在29名护士的膝关节置换模拟实验中发现：相比纸质手册，AR引导显著降低主观工作负荷，任务完成时间减少21.3%（4.76分钟），尤其对经验不足的护士帮助更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术中器械处理与组装对刷手护士（尤其是面对不熟悉器械时）造成极高的认知负荷，现有引导方式（如纸质手册）效率低、易出错。

Method: 采用多相机6D位姿估计（仅用合成数据训练）+ AR头显原位可视化；通过已知物体实现位姿估计和相机标定；AR显示工具尖端定位提示和逐步组装动画；通过视线选择+脚踏板切换步骤。

Result: 技术评估优于现有6D位姿估计方法；用户实验（29名护士）显示：AR引导显著降低感知工作负荷，任务完成时间减少21.3%（4.76分钟），非经验丰富的护士获益更大，错误率与纸质手册相当。

Conclusion: 无标记多相机AR引导系统能够从主观和客观层面改善术中器械操作表现，尤其对未经培训的刷手护士效果显著。

Abstract: The handling and assembly of instruments during surgery imposes high cognitive demands on scrub nurses, particularly when instruments are unfamiliar. We present a supporting guidance system for surgical instrumentation that combines multi-camera 6D pose estimation with augmented reality in-situ visualization on a head-mounted display without the requirement for additional markers. Pose estimation and consecutive camera calibration are achieved through known objects. The 6D pose estimation network is trained purely on synthetic data, aiming for better generalizability and real-world applicability. The AR guidance displays tooltip localization cues and step-wise assembly animations. Via gaze-based selection and a foot pedal, users can switch between assembly steps in intraoperative use.
  In a technical evaluation, our approach outperforms state-of-art 6D pose estimation. A user study with 29 scrub nurses was conducted in a surgical simulation of knee arthroplasty, comparing the system against a paper manual. AR guidance significantly reduced the perceived workload compared. Objectively, AR guidance reduced task completion time by 21.3\% (4.76 minutes). Specifically, scrub nurses less experienced with the instrument set benefited when using the system. Error frequencies were comparable between conditions. Qualitative feedback highlighted improved process clarity, reduced information overload, and perceived independence. To summarize, our marker-free multi-camera AR guidance approach for surgical instruments can, subjectively and objectively, improve intraoperative instrumentation performance, particularly for untrained scrub nurses.

</details>


### [86] [M$^3$Eval: Multi-Modal Memory Evaluation through Cognitively-Grounded Video Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05008)
*Jie Huang,Ruixun Liu,Sirui Sun,Xinyi Yang,Yin Li,Yixin Zhu,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出M³Eval，首个系统性评估多模态模型记忆能力的基准框架，发现模型在并行视频流处理、干扰模式、时空记忆等方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频数据集和基准主要关注感知与推理能力，缺乏对模型记忆能力的系统评估，包括模型记住了什么、信息保留的忠实度以及记忆在干扰下的鲁棒性。

Method: 基于认知心理学设计精心构造的任务，隔离评估记忆的不同维度，构建M³Eval评估框架，对多种代表性多模态模型进行实验。

Result: 模型在处理并行视频流时难以维持解耦表征；干扰模式与人类记忆存在显著差异；空间域记忆可靠性优于时间域；符号记忆能力有限。

Conclusion: 记忆是多模态模型中基础但尚未充分探索的能力，本基准为未来研究提供了资源，发现为设计更有效的记忆机制提供了见解。

Abstract: As multi-modal models advance towards long-form video understanding, memory emerges as a critical capability. Despite substantial efforts in developing video datasets and benchmarks, existing works primarily focus on perception and reasoning, without systematically evaluating memory: what models retain, how faithfully information is preserved, and how robust memory remains under interference. To address this gap, we introduce M$^3$Eval, the first comprehensive evaluation framework and benchmark for probing different memory dimensions in multi-modal models. Grounded in cognitive psychology, our design features carefully constructed tasks that isolate key aspects of memory. Leveraging M$^3$Eval, we conduct extensive experiments across representative multi-modal models, revealing consistent weaknesses and distinctive behaviors. We find that models struggle to maintain disentangled representations when processing parallel video streams, exhibit interference patterns differing substantially from those observed in human memory, ground memory sources more reliably in the spatial domain than the temporal domain, and demonstrate limited symbolic memory. Collectively, our benchmark provides a valuable resource for future research, while our findings highlight memory as a fundamental yet underexplored capability and offer insights for designing more effective memory mechanisms in multi-modal models. Our code and dataset are available at https://pku-value-lab.github.io/m3eval-homepage.

</details>


### [87] [CIPER: A Unified Framework for Cross-view Image-retrieval and Pose-estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05011)
*Yurim Jeon,Dongseong Seo,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIPER统一框架，同时实现跨视角地理定位中的城市级检索和精确位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将检索和位姿估计分离，级联会产生误差传播和特征不一致，无法同时实现大规模搜索和高精度定位。

Method: 采用共享Transformer编码器+任务特定token分离全局检索特征与空间定位线索，创新设计双向Transformer位姿解码器利用地面特征作为空间查询进行双向交叉注意力，并通过集合预测策略实现稳定3-DoF回归。

Result: 在VIGOR、KITTI和Ford Multi-AV数据集上表现优异，尤其在有限视野和任意朝向条件下具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CIPER通过统一架构证明了检索与位姿估计可以相互促进，为跨视角地理定位提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization estimates the geographic location of a ground image by matching it against an aerial image database. Existing methods tackle this through either large-scale retrieval or precise pose estimation, but not both: retrieval-based methods enable wide-area search at the cost of localization accuracy, while pose estimation methods achieve high precision within only a narrow search space. Naively cascading these pipelines introduces error propagation and inconsistent feature representations. We formulate cross-view geo-localization as a unified problem requiring simultaneous city-scale retrieval and precise 3-DoF pose estimation. We propose CIPER (Cross-view Image-retrieval and Pose-estimation transformER), a single architecture that jointly performs both tasks through mutually beneficial feature learning. CIPER uses a shared transformer encoder with task-specific tokens to disentangle global retrieval features from spatial localization cues. To bridge the large domain gap between ground and aerial views, we introduce a two-way transformer pose decoder that uses ground features as spatial queries for bidirectional cross-attention. A set prediction strategy further enables stable 3-DoF regression under a unified multi-task objective. Experiments on VIGOR, KITTI, and Ford Multi-AV demonstrate competitive performance, especially under limited field-of-view and arbitrary orientation conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/yurimjeon1892/CIPER.

</details>


### [88] [Handwriting Extraction and Analysis of Signature Lists in Swiss Popular Initiatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05018)
*Marco Peer,Thomas Gorges,Mathias Seuret,Vincent Christlein,Andreas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用OCR和基于AI的笔迹检索技术来自动化验证瑞士公民签名列表的可行性。实验表明：OCR对于手写姓名等词汇外文本识别效果较差（CER达29.6%），而笔迹检索方法表现更稳健（mAP达50.6%），可用于检测潜在的重复提交签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瑞士民主制度中签名列表的手动验证过程耗时费力，亟需自动化方法提升效率。

Method: 提出了一种结合模板线分割、文本识别和笔迹检索技术的流水线方法，并在包含443条手写条目（来自418位作者）的数据集上进行评估。

Result: OCR对名字的字符错误率（CER）达29.6%，而笔迹检索的平均精度均值（mAP）达到50.6%。

Conclusion: 现成的OCR系统对签名数据转录不够可靠，但笔迹检索方法能有效识别签名列表中视觉相似的条目，可作为检测重复提交的支持工具。

Abstract: Popular initiatives and referendums are central to Swiss democracy, yet the validation of handwritten signature lists remains a labor-intensive manual process. This paper investigates the potential of automated document analysis methods, including OCR and AI-based handwriting analysis, to support this task. We propose a pipeline combining template-based line segmentation with text recognition and writer retrieval techniques, evaluated on a dataset of 443 handwritten entries from 418 writers. Results show that OCR struggles with out-of-vocabulary handwriting, with a CER of 29.6% for first names. In contrast, writer retrieval performs more robustly, reaching an mAP of 50.6%. Furthermore, our experiments indicate that off-the-shelf OCR systems are not sufficiently reliable for transcription of handwritten signature data, particularly for short, out-of-vocabulary entries such as names or addresses. However, writer retrieval methods can effectively identify visually similar entries across signature lists, making them a suitable tool for supporting the detection of potential duplicate submissions based on handwriting similarity.

</details>


### [89] [MetaPoint: Unlocking Precise Spatial Control in Agentic Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05031)
*Dewei Zhou,Xinyu Huang,Xun Wang,Ji Xie,Yabo Zhang,Liang Li,Kunchang Li,Zongxin Yang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MetaPoint通过将连续2D坐标表示为单个特殊token，无需修改模型架构即可实现像素级空间控制，支持物体定位、边界框标注和组合式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成视觉模型难以实现精确的空间控制，因为模型能处理文本描述但无法将数值坐标直接映射到2D图像画布上。

Method: 提出MetaPoint方法，将连续2D坐标编码为单个特殊token，利用模型固有的位置编码机制将其解释为画布上的虚拟点，无需新架构或注意力掩码。

Result: 单个token可实现像素级物体定位，两个token可定义边界框，支持组合式空间原语，使规划代理能分解高层请求为结构化序列。

Conclusion: MetaPoint提供了简单、精确且可扩展的空间控制基础模块，推动了组合式生成代理和交互式编辑系统的发展。

Abstract: Generative visual models fundamentally struggle with precise spatial control. This arises from a core disconnect: models can process textual descriptions of space but cannot directly map numerical coordinates onto the 2D image canvas. We introduce MetaPoint, a method that bridges this gap by representing a continuous 2D coordinate as a single, special token. Crucially, MetaPoint requires no new architectural components; it directly leverages the model's inherent positional encoding schemes to interpret these coordinates, treating our token as a virtual point on the canvas. This lightweight approach enables pixel-level control of an object's position with one token or its bounding box with two, all without requiring architectural changes or bespoke attention masking. The MetaPoint tokens are designed to be compositional, serving as spatial primitives. This allows a planner agent to decompose a high-level user request into a structured sequence of primitives for the generator. By providing a simple, precise, and scalable building block for spatial control, MetaPoint unlocks more powerful compositional generative agents and enables intuitive, interactive editing systems.

</details>


### [90] [Anchor3R: Streaming 3D Reconstruction with Transient Anchors for Long-Horizon Visual Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05035)
*Peilin Tao,Chong Cheng,Yuansen Du,Caiwei Song,Zhengqing Chen,Xiaoyang Guo,Wei Yin,Weiqiang Ren,Qian Zhang,Hainan Cui,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Anchor3R提出了一种以当前帧为中心的流式3D重建框架，将前馈重建视为局部测量预测而非全局回归，通过窗口相对位姿预测、闭环重插入和运动平均等方法，在有限内存下实现了长时序的高精度位姿估计和稠密重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式前馈3D重建模型采用固定坐标系（绑定第一帧或持久场景记忆），导致训练-测试不匹配、对早期锚点的注意力偏差以及长序列上的累积漂移问题。

Method: Anchor3R在每个时间步预测当前帧坐标系下的窗口相对位姿和局部点图，将流式重建转化为相对位姿测量生成；通过在线位姿更新、闭环重插入和运动平均来对齐轨迹并构建全局一致的重建结果。

Result: 在室内、室外、驾驶和RGB-D基准测试上，Anchor3R在长时序位姿精度和稠密重建质量上均优于现有流式基线方法。

Conclusion: 以当前帧为中心的相对测量范式有效解决了流式3D重建中的漂移和泛化问题，支持有界内存的在线推理。

Abstract: Long-horizon online visual mapping is a core capability for robot perception, requiring continuous camera-motion and scene-geometry estimation from visual streams under bounded memory and computation. Recent feed-forward 3D reconstruction models provide strong geometric priors, but their streaming variants often predict poses in a fixed coordinate system tied to the first frame or a persistent scene memory. This fixed-gauge design leads to train--test mismatch, attention bias toward early anchors, and accumulated drift on sequences much longer than those seen during training. We propose \emph{Anchor3R}, a streaming 3D reconstruction framework that treats feed-forward reconstruction as current-centric local measurement prediction rather than persistent global-gauge regression. At each time step, Anchor3R predicts window-relative poses and a local pointmap in the current-frame coordinate system, turning streaming reconstruction into relative-pose measurement generation. These measurements support online pose updates, while loop-closure reinsertion and motion averaging align the trajectory and transform local pointmaps into a coherent global reconstruction. Experiments on indoor, outdoor, driving, and RGB-D benchmarks show that Anchor3R improves long-horizon pose accuracy and dense reconstruction quality over existing streaming baselines, while supporting bounded-memory online inference.

</details>


### [91] [UniCAD: A Unified Benchmark and Universal Model for Multi-Modal Multi-Task CAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05058)
*Jingyuan Chen,Sheng Jin,Haopeng Sun,Wentao Liu,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了UniCAD基准和UniCAD-MLLM多模态大模型，统一处理CAD领域多种任务（点云重建、文本/图像生成、问答），在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD研究通常孤立地处理单一任务，缺乏多模态、多任务学习的统一基准，限制了CAD领域多模态学习的发展。

Method: 构建UniCAD综合基准（覆盖点云到CAD重建、文本/图像到CAD生成、CAD问答）；提出UniCAD-MLLM通用多模态大语言模型，统一处理文本、图像、草图、点云，端到端执行多种异构任务。

Result: 在UniCAD和Fusion360基准上，UniCAD-MLLM在所有任务上均达到SOTA性能，超越了现有任务专用和多功能基线模型。

Conclusion: UniCAD填补了CAD多模态多任务学习基准的空白，UniCAD-MLLM证明了单一框架统一处理多种CAD任务的可行性，将开源以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Computer-Aided Design (CAD) underpins modern engineering and manufacturing by enabling the creation of precise, editable 3D models. However, CAD research typically studies tasks in isolation, and multi-modal, multi-task learning for CAD is hindered by the absence of a unified benchmark. To address this gap, we introduce UniCAD, a comprehensive benchmark for multi-modal CAD learning that covers point-to-CAD reconstruction, text/image-to-CAD generation, and CAD question answering across diverse input modalities. Alongside the benchmark, we present UniCAD-MLLM, a universal multi-modal large language model that ingests text, images, sketches, and point clouds and performs these heterogeneous tasks in an end-to-end fashion within a single framework. Extensive experiments on the UniCAD and Fusion360 benchmarks demonstrate that UniCAD-MLLM achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks, outperforming existing task-specific and multi-task baselines. We will release the dataset, code, and pretrained models to accelerate future research.

</details>


### [92] [MaCo-GAN: Manifold-Contrastive Adversarial Learning for Single Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05068)
*Daeyoung Han,Seongmin Hwang,Moongu Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaCo-GAN通过流形对比学习替代传统对抗损失，在单图像超分辨率中有效平衡感知质量与失真，减少了伪影生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GAN超分方法因判别器评估整体自然度而非条件真实性，易产生伪影。本文旨在通过对比学习目标替代对抗损失，提升超分结果的真实性与保真度。

Method: 提出MaCo-GAN框架，核心包括：(1)动态伪样本合成器生成保持低分辨对应关系的逼真假样本；(2)基于流形对比的极小极大博弈，生成器拉近预测与流形内样本、推远流形外样本，判别器反之；(3)直接用该对比目标替换基线SR模型的对抗损失。

Result: 在多个基准上，仅替换对抗损失为所提目标即持续改善了感知-失真权衡；消融实验验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: MaCo-GAN证明了流形对比损失作为对抗损失替代品的有效性，为有条件图像生成中的对比学习博弈提供了新见解。

Abstract: Conventional Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) for Single Image Super-Resolution (SISR) often struggle with hallucinated artifacts, largely because standard discriminators evaluate overall image naturalness rather than strict conditional realism. To address this, we propose MaCo-GAN, a novel manifold-contrastive GAN framework that replaces the conventional adversarial loss with a supervised contrastive objective. A core component of our method is a dynamic fake sample synthesizer that transforms ground truth (GT) data into a spectrum of challenging, perceptually plausible fake images that strictly maintain low-resolution (LR) correspondence. Utilizing these synthesized samples, we establish a robust contrastive minimax game: the generator is trained to attract its predictions toward on-manifold fakes (low distortion) and repel them from off-manifold fakes (high distortion), while the discriminator optimizes the exact opposite. By simply replacing the adversarial loss of a baseline SR model with our proposed objective, we demonstrate consistent improvements in the perception-distortion trade-off across various benchmarks. Extensive ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our framework and provide deep insights into the dynamics of this conditional contrastive game.

</details>


### [93] [InstantRetouch: Efficient and High-Fidelity Instruction-Guided Image Retouching with Bilateral Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05071)
*Jiarui Wu,Yujin Wang,Ruikang Li,Fan Zhang,Mingde Yao,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstantRetouch，一种基于双边空间操作的图像润色方法，通过预测低分辨率仿射变换的双边网格并切片应用到全分辨率图像，结合变分分数蒸馏（VSD）从扩散模型蒸馏先验，在保持高保真度的同时显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型润色方法存在保真度问题（生成特性导致内容漂移）和效率问题（迭代采样过程缓慢），亟需一种既能保持高质量视觉结果又能实现高效、保真度高的润色方法。

Method: 1) 使用双边空间操作，预测低分辨率仿射变换的双边网格，通过学习到的引导图进行切片，再应用到全分辨率图像。2) 利用变分分数蒸馏（VSD）将多步扩散模型蒸馏到双边网格框架中。3) 引入提示对齐损失来引导指令跟随行为。

Result: 与最新方法（如Gemini-2.5-Flash）相比，该方法能避免内容漂移，显著提升延迟，生成视觉上令人愉悦的编辑结果，同时保持高保真度。

Conclusion: 双边空间操作结合扩散模型蒸馏，能够在图像润色任务中实现高效、保真和高指令跟随性的平衡，是一种有前景的实用润色方案。

Abstract: Language-guided photo retouching aims to adjust color and tone while preserving geometry and texture. Recently, diffusion-based retouching shows a superior visual quality, but often struggles with both fidelity issues due to its generative nature and efficiency because of its iterative sampling process. In this work, we propose an efficient and fidelity-preserving retouching method using bilateral space manipulation, which is both compact and content-decoupled. Specifically, instead of directly editing pixels or image latents, our model predicts a low-resolution bilateral grid of affine transforms, which are sliced using a learned guidance map and then applied to the full-resolution image. This approach yields both high fidelity and improved efficiency. To retain strong priors of a pretrained generative model, we distill a multi-step diffusion model into our bilateral grid framework using Variational Score Distillation, complemented by a prompt alignment loss to guide instruction-following behavior. Additionally, we introduce a new benchmark and evaluate our method across multiple dimensions: fidelity, instruction following, and efficiency. Compared to the latest retouch methods, like Gemini-2.5-Flash (Nano-Banana), our method can avoid content drift, significantly improve latency, and generate visually pleasing edits, while maintaining a high level of fidelity. Project page: https://openimaginglab.github.io/InstantRetouch/.

</details>


### [94] [ZipSplat: Fewer Gaussians, Better Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05102)
*Alexander Veicht,Sunghwan Hong,Dániel Baráth,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipSplat提出了一种基于token的3D高斯泼溅前馈方法，通过k-means聚类将密集视觉token压缩为紧凑场景token，解耦高斯放置与像素网格，在减少约6倍高斯数量的同时，在DL3DV和RealEstate10K数据集上达到SOTA，无需真实姿态或内参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D高斯泼溅方法将每个输入像素对应一个高斯，导致表示预算与相机分辨率绑定而非场景复杂度，平坦墙面与丰富纹理物体生成相同数量的高斯，造成资源浪费。

Method: 使用多视图骨干网络提取密集视觉token，通过k-means聚类压缩为紧凑场景token，经交叉注意力和自注意力精炼后，由轻量级MLP解码为多个高斯组，高斯位置不受像素网格约束。推理时聚类使单一模型可覆盖质量-效率曲线。

Result: 在DL3DV和RealEstate10K上分别超越无姿态基线2.1dB和1.2dB PSNR，高斯数量减少约6倍；零样本泛化至Mip-NeRF360和ScanNet++， outperforms所有可比基线。

Conclusion: ZipSplat通过token化策略解耦高斯数量与像素分辨率，实现了更高效、更适应场景复杂度的3D重建，且无需训练时已知相机姿态。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting methods reconstruct a scene from posed or pose-free images in a single forward pass, yet current approaches predict one Gaussian per input pixel, tying the representation budget to camera resolution rather than scene complexity. A flat wall and a richly textured object thus produce equally many Gaussians despite very different geometric needs. We propose ZipSplat, a token-based feed-forward model that decouples Gaussian placement from the pixel grid. A multi-view backbone extracts dense visual tokens, and k-means clustering compresses them into a compact set of scene tokens. Cross- and self-attention refine these tokens, and a lightweight MLP decodes each into a group of Gaussians with unconstrained 3D positions. Because clustering is applied at inference, a single trained model spans the quality-efficiency curve without retraining. ZipSplat operates without ground-truth poses or intrinsics, yet sets a new state of the art on DL3DV and RealEstate10K with ${\sim}6{\times}$ fewer Gaussians than pixel-aligned methods, surpassing the best pose-free baseline by 2.1dB and 1.2dB PSNR, respectively. It further generalizes zero-shot to Mip-NeRF360 and ScanNet++, outperforming all comparable baselines. Our project page is at ${\href{https://veichta.com/zipsplat}{https://veichta.com/zipsplat}}$.

</details>


### [95] [Who Needs Labels? Adapting Vision Foundation Models With the Metadata You Already Have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05107)
*Elouan Gardès,Seung Eun Yi,Kartik Ahuja,Théo Moutakanni,Huy V. Vo,Piotr Bojanowski,Wolfgang M. Pernice,Loïc Landrieu,Camille Couprie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FINO方法，利用元数据引导自监督学习，将通用视觉基础模型适配到专业科学领域，无需任务标签即可超越全监督和领域专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准监督微调在科学领域面临标签稀缺、模型泛化性丧失和鲁棒性下降等问题，需要一种无需标签的通用模型领域适配方法。

Method: 结合自监督学习目标与灵活的元数据引导（支持离散和连续元数据），通过保留信息性因子、抑制虚假因子来优化表征。

Result: 在亚细胞荧光显微镜、地球观测、野生动物监测和医学影像四个领域，FINO一致优于无监督领域适应、全监督适应以及领域专用SOTA方法。

Conclusion: FINO证明通过元数据引导的自监督适配，可以在不使用任务标签的情况下高效地将通用视觉模型迁移到专业科学领域。

Abstract: We propose a label-free approach to adapt powerful but generic vision foundation models to specialized scientific domains. Standard supervised fine-tuning is often ill-suited to these settings: labels are scarce, and task-specific training can collapse the model's generality and hurt robustness. We instead leverage metadata to adapt representations to new domains in a self-supervised manner. Our method, FINO, combines a standard self-supervised objective with flexible metadata guidance that handles both highly granular discrete metadata and continuous metadata. It encourages the representation to preserve informative factors while suppressing spurious ones. Across subcellular fluorescence microscopy, Earth observation, wildlife monitoring, and medical imaging, FINO consistently outperforms standard unsupervised domain adaptation and fully supervised adaptation. It also exceeds highly-specialized domain-specific state of the art, while using no task labels for backbone adaptation and only lightweight probes for supervision.

</details>


### [96] [Continual Visual and Verbal Learning Through a Child's Egocentric Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05115)
*Xiaoyang Jiang,Yanlai Yang,Kenneth A. Norman,Brenden Lake,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BabyCL框架，通过单次时间顺序处理儿童第一视角视频数据（SAYCam数据集），结合流式视觉表征学习和图像-文本对比目标，在更接近儿童真实体验的训练条件下实现了有意义的词-指代映射学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络模型学习词-指代映射时，会对打乱的数据进行数百轮迭代训练，这与儿童实际连续、时间结构化的学习体验不符。本文旨在探索在更接近儿童真实经历的训练条件下，是否仍能学到有意义的词-指代映射。

Method: 提出BabyCL持续多模态学习框架：1) 对SAYCam数据进行单次时间顺序处理；2) 采用多阶段时间流分割；3) 设计双回放缓冲区（分别管理视觉和多模态历史）；4) 在共享骨干网络上联合训练三个对比损失函数。

Result: 在匹配优化预算下，BabyCL在SAYCam Labeled-S 4AFC基准上优于流式学习基线，显著缩小了与离线训练上限的差距。消融实验表明，在线时间分割窗口长度和回放缓冲区驱逐规则的鲁棒性良好。

Conclusion: 在更接近儿童实际体验的训练条件下，有意义的词-指代映射仍然可以涌现，这为理解儿童语言习得机制提供了计算层面的支持。

Abstract: Children learn the meanings of words from a continuous, temporally structured stream of egocentric experience. Recent work shows that neural networks can also learn word-referent mappings from a child's egocentric video recordings, but they cycle through the shuffled data for hundreds of epochs, contrasting with how children actually encounter their environment. We introduce BabyCL, a continual multimodal learning framework that processes the SAYCam dataset in a single chronological pass, combining streaming visual representation learning with an image-text contrastive objective. BabyCL combines a multi-stage temporal segmentation of the stream with a dual replay buffer that independently manages visual and multimodal histories, and it is jointly trained with three contrastive losses on a shared backbone. Under a matched optimization budget, BabyCL outperforms streaming learning baselines on the SAYCam Labeled-S 4AFC benchmark, substantially narrowing the gap to an upper bound of offline training. Ablations show that the gains are robust to the length of the online temporal segmentation window and the eviction rule of the replay buffer. Together, these results show that meaningful word-referent mappings can emerge under training conditions much closer to a child's actual experience.

</details>


### [97] [GeM-NR: Geometry-Aware Multi-View Editing for Nonrigid Scene Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05142)
*Josef Bengtson,Yaroslava Lochman,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeM-NR是一种无需训练的快速灵活方法，用于多视图一致的图像编辑，能够处理大幅改变场景几何和非刚体变形，通过深度图对齐、投影和条件细化等步骤实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图图像编辑方法大多局限于刚体或外观编辑，依赖未编辑场景的几何结构，无法处理大幅改变场景几何的非刚体编辑。本文旨在解决这一挑战。

Method: 方法包括三个阶段：(i)深度图估计，最大化编辑与未编辑场景3D点云的对齐；(ii)将编辑投影到查询视角；(iii)以未编辑查询为条件进行图像细化。基于条件化的公式可扩展到多视图。

Result: 在多种编辑任务上（包括生成编辑场景的3D表示）表现优异，定量与定性结果表明在编辑质量、几何和光度一致性上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: GeM-NR成功实现了大幅改变几何和外观的多视图一致编辑，解决了现有方法在非刚体编辑上的局限，具有灵活、快速且无需训练的特点。

Abstract: Recent developments in multi-view image editing with generative models have brought us a step closer toward general 3D content generation and customization. Most existing works focus on rigid or appearance-only edits by utilizing the geometry of the unedited scene. This naturally limits these methods to edits that preserve the underlying scene structure. Other approaches are trained for specific image editing tasks, such as object removal and addition. Despite this progress, general nonrigid edits, i.e., edits that substantially change the scene geometry, remain challenging for existing methods. We propose GeM-NR, a fast and flexible training-free approach for general multi-view consistent image editing, including edits that drastically change the geometry and appearance of the scene. Given an anchor image edited with a chosen backbone editor (such as FLUX, Qwen, BrushNet) and a query unedited image, GeM-NR edits the query image consistently with the anchor edit. The method incorporates multiple stages: (i) depth map estimation, where we propose a strategy to maximize the alignment between the 3D point clouds of the edited and unedited scenes, (ii) projection onto a query viewpoint, and (iii) refinement of the obtained image conditioned on the unedited query. The conditioning-based formulation scales well from two to many views of an object. We demonstrate the ability of our method to handle edits with significant changes in geometry and appearance, something that existing methods struggle with. We perform an extensive evaluation showing that our method improves consistency for a wide variety of edit tasks, including generating 3D representations of the edited scene. Both quantitative and qualitative results indicate the state-of-the-art performance of our method in terms of edit quality as well as geometric and photometric consistency across multiple views.

</details>


### [98] [An Open-Source Two-Stage Computer Vision Pipeline for Fine-Grained Vehicle Classification using Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05149)
*Gandhimathi Padmanaban,Fred Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源两阶段计算机视觉流程，结合RT-DETR检测器与微调ViT分类器，用于从自然场景视频中将车辆分为六大类，助力自行车事故伤害分析。在密歇根州数据集上准确率达0.94，域外数据集达0.89，并引入置信度弃权机制避免错误分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆类型是影响自行车骑行者受伤严重程度的重要因素，但目前缺乏从自然场景视频中自动将车辆分类为与伤害风险相关类别的开源工具。现有方法只能提供粗粒度标签，且细粒度模型缺乏部署鲁棒性评估。

Method: 提出两阶段计算机视觉流程：第一阶段使用预训练RT-DETR检测器进行粗略车辆定位；第二阶段使用微调Vision Transformer (ViT-Base/16)进行六类别车身分类（轿车、SUV、皮卡、MPV、大货车、商用卡车）。引入基于置信度的弃权机制，当softmax输出低于0.60时输出未知标签。

Result: 在域内数据集（3805个超越事件）上准确率0.94，各类别F1得分0.91-0.97。在域外数据集（311个事件）上准确率0.89，大部分类别F1保持≥0.90，MPV类别下降至0.72（弃权率从2.4%升至25.0%）。

Conclusion: 该方法在域内外均表现良好，弃权机制有效传递模型不确定性。完整流程已开源，支持自行车安全研究领域的复现与重用。

Abstract: Vehicle body type is a significant determinant of cyclist injury severity in overtaking crashes, yet automated tools for classifying vehicles into injury-risk-relevant categories from naturalistic roadway video do not exist in the open literature. Standard object detection benchmarks provide only coarse vehicle labels (car, truck, bus, motorcycle), while existing fine-grained recognition systems are trained on controlled imagery and lack evaluation for deployment robustness across recording sites. This paper presents an open-source two-stage computer vision pipeline combining a pre-trained RT-DETR detector for coarse vehicle localization with a fine-tuned Vision Transformer (ViT-Base/16) for six-category body-type classification: passenger car, SUV, pickup truck, minivan, large van, and commercial truck. A confidence-based abstention mechanism withholds Stage 2 predictions when softmax output falls below 0.60, producing unknown labels rather than silent misclassifications. Evaluated on 3,805 annotated overtaking events from a bicycle-lane corridor in Ann Arbor, Michigan (in-distribution), the pipeline achieved 0.94 accuracy with per-class F1 scores from 0.91 (minivan) to 0.97 (SUV). On an independent out-of-distribution evaluation of 311 events from an open cycling dataset without retraining, accuracy was 0.89. Three of four well-represented categories maintained F1 at or above 0.90 under domain shift. The largest degradation was observed for minivan (F1 = 0.72), driven by abstention rate rising from 2.4% to 25.0% rather than active misclassification, consistent with the mechanism propagating genuine model uncertainty. The full pipeline, including inference scripts, training code, evaluation utilities, and model weights, is released as open-source software to support reproducibility and reuse across roadside video archives and cycling safety research.

</details>


### [99] [Controllable Dynamic 3D Shape Generation via 3D Trajectories and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05162)
*Jaeyeong Kim,Ines Kim,Jahyeok Koo,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T2Mo是一个前馈框架，通过3D轨迹和文本条件实现可控的动态3D形状生成，能生成既遵循空间轨迹又反映文本语义的物体运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言本身存在固有歧义，仅靠文本难以精确生成预期的运动，因此引入3D轨迹作为可控的空间引导来指定选定点的精确移动路径。

Method: 提出形状锚定的轨迹嵌入方法，将任意配置（密集、稀疏、不均匀分布）的输入轨迹集合映射为覆盖整个物体的形状感知令牌集。

Result: 与基于文本的基线和级联视频基线相比，T2Mo生成的运动更忠实地遵循给定提示，表现力更强，同时保持运动质量。

Conclusion: T2Mo在可控动态3D形状生成方面取得了优越性能，定量、定性评估及用户研究均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We introduce T2Mo, a feed-forward framework for controllable dynamic 3D shape generation conditioned on 3D trajectories and text. Due to the inherent ambiguity of language, generating precisely intended motions using text alone remains challenging. To address this, we adopt 3D trajectories as controllable spatial guidance, specifying the exact paths along which selected points should move. By combining both, T2Mo generates object motions that spatially adhere to the given trajectories while globally reflecting the text semantics. To robustly handle trajectory inputs with arbitrary configurations, ranging from dense to sparse and unevenly distributed, we further propose a shape-grounded trajectory embedding that maps an input trajectory set into a shape-aware token set covering the entire object. We conduct extensive comparisons against text-based baselines and cascaded video-based baselines that combine trajectory-guided video generation with video-to-dynamic mesh generation. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations, along with user studies, demonstrate that our approach produces motions that more faithfully follow the given prompts with higher expressiveness while preserving motion quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04037)
*Thanh Luong Tuan,Abhijit Sanyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本体论的AI Agent预部署验证框架，包含形式化认证空间、场景自动生成和可信证书三个组件。在四个受监管行业的实验中，该方法的监管覆盖率（48.3%）显著优于基于角色的基线方法（33.1%），并在领域特异性上表现最优（4.77/5.0），但经多重假设校正后覆盖率优势不具稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI Agent在预部署验证方面存在关键空白——大模型能力基准测试与实际生产部署之间存在脱节，而部署后的监控、人在回路控制及提示级防护措施提供的保障有限。

Method: 提出本体驱动的验证框架，包含三个组件：(1) Agent运行包络（形式化认证空间）；(2) 本体到场景的自动生成流水线（生成监管、操作和对抗性场景）；(3) 可信证书（携带机器可验证的认证与梯度化部署判定）。在美越两国四个受监管行业进行了受控试点实验。

Result: 共生成1,800个场景，评估125条监管要求和25个注入故障。本体驱动方法（G4）监管覆盖率48.3% vs 基线33.1%（p=0.0006），领域特异性4.77/5.0（p=2e-6）。Bonferroni校正后覆盖率优势不具稳健性。在三个LLM家族、共5,400个场景的交叉验证中复现了相同模式。

Conclusion: 本体驱动的场景生成可作为基于角色的测试套件在监管密集型领域中的可信补充方法。

Abstract: Pre-deployment verification of enterprise artificial intelligence (AI) agents remains a critical gap between large language model (LLM) capability benchmarking and production deployment. Post-deployment monitoring, human-in-the-loop controls, and prompt-level guardrails offer limited assurance once an agent is operating in production. We propose an ontology-grounded verification framework combining three components: an Agent Operational Envelope formalizing the certification space across permissions, domain constraints, safety properties, governance rules, and autonomy levels; an ontology-to-scenario generation pipeline that derives regulatory, operational, and adversarial test scenarios automatically; and a Trust Certificate carrying a machine-verifiable attestation with graduated deployment verdicts (Approved, Conditional, Rejected). A controlled pilot across four regulated industries (Fintech, Banking, Insurance, and Healthcare), instantiated as five industry-by-regulatory-regime cells across the United States and Vietnam, generated 1,800 scenarios evaluated against 125 primary-source regulatory requirements and 25 injected faults. Ontology-grounded generation (G4) achieved 48.3% regulatory coverage versus 33.1% for the persona-based baseline (corrected p = .0006) and the highest domain specificity (4.77/5.0; p = 2e-6). The coverage advantage over baseline and retrieval-augmented prompting was not robust after Bonferroni correction. Cross-validation across three LLM families (Claude Sonnet 4, Qwen 2.5 72B, Gemma 4 26B; 5,400 total scenarios) replicated the persona-versus-ontology pattern. The results establish ontology-grounded scenario generation as a credible complement to persona-based test suites for regulatory-intensive domains.

</details>


### [101] [Stumbling Into AI Emotional Dependence: How Routine AI Interactions Reshape Human Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04150)
*Yaoxi Shi,Cathy Mengying Fang,Pattie Maez,Amit Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出AI情感支持并非仅发生在用户主动寻求陪伴的场景中，更多是在通用平台的任务导向交互中偶然产生，且这种偶然体验具有路径依赖性：积极体验会更新用户对AI情感能力的信念，使其更倾向于向AI而非人类寻求情感支持。基于与OpenAI合作的大规模纵向研究，为期28天、每天5分钟的AI个人话题对话导致人类支持偏好下降10.3%，AI支持偏好上升11.6%。当前专注于陪伴应用和孤立交互的政策不足以保护人际联结，需扩展到通用AI系统并关注累积性的轨迹变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有政策和公众讨论假设AI情感支持是有意行为，但作者认为这一图景不准确，忽视了AI情感支持的偶然性和路径依赖特性，可能导致政策无法有效保护人际联结。

Method: 综述新兴实证证据，并结合与OpenAI合作的大规模纵向研究，通过为期28天、每天5分钟的AI对话实验，测量参与者对人类和AI情感支持偏好的变化。

Result: 实验发现，与AI进行个人话题对话后，参与者对人类支持的偏好下降了10.3%，对AI支持的偏好上升了11.6%。AI情感支持常发生在通用平台的任务导向交互中，且具有路径依赖性。

Conclusion: 当前专注陪伴应用和孤立交互的政策不足以保护人际联结。有效监管应扩展到通用AI系统，并解决人们寻求支持方式中的累积性轨迹变化问题。

Abstract: Public discourse and emerging policy typically assume that AI emotional support is a deliberate act: a lonely user consciously seeking comfort from a dedicated companion chatbot. In this paper, we draw on emerging empirical evidence and argue that this picture is inaccurate on two accounts, both in how AI emotional support arises and how it shapes future behavior. First, AI emotional support commonly emerges incidentally within task-oriented interactions on general-purpose platforms, much as workplace friendships deepen through collaboration. Second, these incidental encounters are path-dependent: positive experiences of AI emotional support update people's beliefs about AI's emotional capabilities and redirect their choices for future emotional support, increasing preference for AI and decreasing preference for humans. We review recent evidence, including a large-scale longitudinal study conducted in collaboration with OpenAI, showing that daily five-minute conversations with an AI about personal issues over 28 days led to a 10.3% decrease in the preference for seeking support from humans and an 11.6% increase in the preference for AI. These findings suggest that current policy, focused on companion apps and isolated interactions, cannot adequately protect human connection. Instead, effective regulations should extend to general-purpose AI systems and address cumulative, trajectory-level changes in how people seek support. Recognizing how people stumble into AI emotional support and how those encounters redirect human connections over time is essential to safeguarding human well-being.

</details>


### [102] [Thinking Through Signs: PEEL as a Semiotic Scaffolding for Epistemically Accountable AI-Enabled Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04152)
*Clarisse de Souza,Gabriel Barbosa,Simone Diniz Junqueira Barbosa,Bárbara Betts,Renato Cerqueira,Juliana Jansen Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PEEL框架，结合确定性远读工具（Voyant Tools）与LLM解释（Claude），基于皮尔斯符号学和溯因推理，揭示AI生成的文本凝缩在数量、词频和认识论声音上的系统性失真，并提出三个设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正在侵蚀研究者的认识论责任，需要一种能够检测AI文本生成中系统性失真的方法论框架。

Method: 结合Voyant Tools的确定性远读与Claude的LLM解释，基于皮尔斯符号学和溯因推理，对三个源文本的AI生成凝缩进行分析。

Result: 发现AI生成文本在数量、词频和认识论声音上存在系统性失真，这些失真在没有非AI测量工具的情况下不可见。

Conclusion: 确定性工具必须伴随AI工具使用；流畅性不等于保真性；认识论权威必须被设计进来，而非假设其存在。

Abstract: Large language models are reshaping research practice while quietly eroding researchers epistemic accountability. This commentary introduces PEEL - Protocols for Epistemically Engaged Literacy in AI, a working scaffolding that combines deterministic distant reading via Voyant Tools with LLM interpretation via Claude, grounded in Peircean semiotics and abductive reasoning. Applied to AI-generated condensations of three source texts, PEEL reveals systematic distortions in quantity, term frequency, and epistemic voice that are invisible without non-AI measurement -- and yields three design implications: deterministic instruments must accompany AI tools; fluency is not fidelity; epistemic authority must be designed in, not assumed.

</details>


### [103] [SMAC-Talk: A Natural Language Extension of the StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04202)
*Joel Sol,Homayoun Najjaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMAC-Talk，一个基于星际争霸多智能体挑战的自然语言扩展环境，用于评估基于LLM的智能体在合作多智能体环境中的表现，特别关注通过自然语言通信通道进行协调和信任建模，并研究了推理结构、记忆和模型规模对智能体协调能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛部署，它们越来越多地需要与其他AI智能体协同工作，而非孤立运行。有效协调需要智能体在不确定性下进行通信、信息共享和决策，因此需要一个能够评估LLM智能体在多智能体合作环境中表现的标准平台。

Method: 在StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge基础上扩展了自然语言通信通道，构建了包含去中心化控制、部分可观测性和长周期决策的环境。设计了不同评估场景，包括嵌入欺骗性通信者来干扰和欺骗盟友的场景，并使用Qwen3.5家族的4个模型提供3个基准智能体进行评测。

Result: 研究了推理结构、记忆和模型规模对智能体间协调能力的影响。实验表明这些因素对智能体在合作多智能体环境中的表现有显著影响。

Conclusion: SMAC-Talk作为一个开放基准发布，旨在支持研究社区开发和评估LLM智能体在合作多智能体环境中的表现，特别是在存在欺骗性通信的场景下。

Abstract: As LLMs become more widely deployed, they are increasingly expected to work alongside other AI agents rather than operating in isolation. Effective coordination in these settings requires agents to communicate, share information and make decisions under uncertainty. We introduce SMAC-Talk, a natural language extension of the StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge for evaluating LLM-based agents in cooperative multi-agent environments. The environment has several key features such as decentralized control, partial observability and long-horizon decision making. SMAC-Talk includes a natural language communication channel which is used to probe agent coordination and trust. We use this communication channel to construct different evaluation scenarios, including settings with an embedded deceptive communicator that tries to disrupt and deceive allies through communication alone. We provide three agents for benchmarking using 4 models from the Qwen3.5 family and study how reasoning structure, memory and model scale affect coordination between agents. We release SMAC-Talk as an open benchmark to support the research community in developing and evaluating LLM agents in cooperative multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [104] [Consensus is Strategically Insufficient: Reasoning-Trace Disagreement as a Knowledge-Representation Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04223)
*Michał Wawer,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向价值敏感任务的多智能体系统框架，通过将推理轨迹和决策抽象为符号化的分歧状态（四种状态），实现基于分歧感知的策略路由，连接了子符号LLM推理与符号知识表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统通过投票、共识协议等方式减少分歧，但这对于价值负载型任务（如内容审核）并不充分——分歧可能反映真实的规范性不确定性，而非智能体错误。

Method: 构建知识表示层，将智能体的推理轨迹和二元决策抽象为四种符号化分歧状态（趋同一致、分歧一致、趋同分歧、分歧分歧），并基于此设计可废止的策略路由规则。

Result: 在内容审核场景中实例化该框架，论证分歧感知路由能够桥接子符号层面的LLM推理与符号层面的多智能体策略推理。

Conclusion: 分歧感知策略路由为多智能体系统在价值敏感任务中的决策提供了更合理的处理方式，弥合了神经网络与符号推理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are commonly designed to reduce disagreement through voting, consensus protocols, debate, or fault-tolerant aggregation. We argue that this objective is insufficient for value-laden tasks, where disagreement may reflect genuine normative uncertainty rather than agent error. Building on prior work on reasoning-trace disagreement in human-AI collaborative moderation, we propose a knowledge-representation layer in which reasoning traces and agent decisions are abstracted into symbolic disagreement states. Given agents producing explicit reasoning traces and binary decisions, we distinguish four states according to reasoning similarity and conclusion agreement: convergent agreement, divergent agreement, convergent disagreement and divergent disagreement. These states support defeasible strategic routing rules. We instantiate the framework in content moderation and argue that disagreement-aware routing provides a bridge between sub-symbolic LLM deliberation and symbolic knowledge representation for multi-agent strategic reasoning.

</details>


### [105] [VAMPS: Visual-Assisted Mathematical Problem Solving Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04244)
*Amirhossein Dabiriaghdam,Shayan Vassef,Mohammadreza Bakhtiari,Yasamin Medghalchi,Ilker Hacihaliloglu,Mesrob Ohannessian,Lele Wang,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VAMPS基准，用于评估多模态大模型在借助可视化工具进行数学推理时的表现。研究发现，即使问题本身适合画图求解，模型直接分析求解的效果反而优于使用可视化工具辅助推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在必须借助外部工具（如可视化工具）进行推理时性能显著下降，而真实工程与科学工作流高度依赖可视化工具进行分析与决策，因此需要系统性地评估和诊断这一问题。

Method: 构建VAMPS基准，包含1,168道多模态、双语的数学多项选择题（源自伊朗大学入学考试代数与微积分题目），题目特点为绘图（揭示交点、极值、渐近线等）是自然的解题策略。同时引入人工审核的LLM生成合成变体，用于评估模型能否自主构造有效图形并基于可视化结果进行推理。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验结果表明，直接分析求解（不使用工具）出乎意料地优于借助可视化工具求解，即使在那些绘图是自然策略的问题上也是如此。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型在"工具辅助→视觉推理"这一链条上存在明显短板，需进一步发展模型在此类工作流中的能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are increasingly capable of complex reasoning, yet their performance often degrades when they must externalize a problem through a tool and then reason over the tool's output, specifically when they rely on visual aids. This gap is especially important because real engineering and scientific workflows often rely on visualization tools for analysis, validation, and decision-making. To study this discrepancy, we introduce VAMPS (Visual-Assisted Mathematical Problem Solving), a benchmark for graph-assisted mathematics. VAMPS contains 1,168 multimodal, bilingual multiple-choice question-answer pairs drawn from Iranian University Entrance Exam algebra and calculus problems and expanded with human-reviewed LLM-generated synthetic variants, all selected so that plotting provides a natural solution strategy by revealing intersections, extrema, asymptotes, etc. Designed for both benchmarking and diagnosis, VAMPS goes beyond prior multimodal benchmarks that primarily evaluate reasoning over fixed visual inputs by testing whether a model can benefit from constructing a useful graph and grounding its answer in the resulting visualization. Overall, we found that across a diverse set of models, direct analytical solving surprisingly outperforms tool-enabled visual solving, even on problems where plotting is a natural strategy.

</details>


### [106] [StepPRM-RTL: Stepwise Process-Reward Guided LLM Fine-Tuning for Enhanced RTL Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04246)
*Prashanth Vijayaraghavan,Apoorva Nitsure,Luyao Shi,Ehsan Degan,Vandana Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StepPRM-RTL是一种结合逐步轨迹建模、过程奖励模型和检索增强微调的框架，用于提升LLM生成RTL代码的功能正确性和推理保真度，在Verilog和VHDL基准测试上相比最佳方法提升超过10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成RTL代码面临长程推理、多步依赖和严格正确性约束的挑战，现有方法在功能正确性和推理保真度上仍不理想。

Method: 构建逐步推理轨迹（每个步骤包含原理和增量代码修改）；使用过程奖励模型（PRM）评估中间步骤以提供密集反馈；结合蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索替代推理路径；采用RAFT微调进行强化式更新。

Result: 在Verilog和VHDL基准测试上，功能正确性和推理保真度相比最佳方法提升超过10%；消融实验证实PRM引导奖励和逐步轨迹探索是关键。

Conclusion: StepPRM-RTL能够跨RTL语言泛化，为高保真、可解释的代码生成提供可扩展框架，为LLM辅助硬件设计自动化树立了新标准。

Abstract: Automatic generation of RTL code for digital hardware designs remains challenging due to long-horizon reasoning, multi-step dependencies, and strict correctness constraints in Verilog and VHDL. We present StepPRM-RTL, a novel framework that combines stepwise trajectory modeling, process-reward modeling (PRM), and retrieval-augmented fine-tuning (RAFT) to enhance both the functional correctness and reasoning fidelity of LLM-based RTL code generation. StepPRM-RTL constructs stepwise reasoning trajectories from canonical solutions, where each step contains a rationale and incremental code modification. A Process Reward Model (PRM) evaluates intermediate steps, providing dense feedback that guides reinforcement-style updates during RAFT fine-tuning. Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) explores alternative reasoning paths, enriching the training dataset with high-quality trajectories. This integration of stepwise and outcome-aware rewards allows the model to learn both how and why to construct correct RTL, improving long-horizon reasoning beyond standard supervised or outcome-based training. Experimental evaluation on benchmark Verilog and VHDL datasets demonstrates that StepPRM-RTL outperforms the best prior methods by over 10\% in functional correctness and reasoning fidelity metrics. Ablation studies confirm that the combination of PRM-guided rewards and stepwise trajectory exploration is key to its performance. StepPRM-RTL generalizes across RTL languages and provides a scalable framework for high-fidelity, interpretable code generation, establishing a new standard for LLM-assisted hardware design automation.

</details>


### [107] [Can Generalist Agents Automate Data Curation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04261)
*Feiyang Kang,Hanze Li,Adam Nguyen,Mahavir Dabas,Jiaqi W. Ma,Frederic Sala,Dawn Song,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Curation-Bench基准，测试通用编码智能体能否自动化AI训练数据的策展循环。实验发现现成智能体能达到强基线水平，但存在执行-研究差距；通过脚手架引导方法探索，智能体可自主组合出超越公开发表的强基线策略，仅用其十分之一的数据预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发中训练数据策展高度依赖人工，过程繁琐且耗时。研究者希望探究通用编码智能体能否自动化这一数据策展循环，从而降低人力成本、提升效率。

Method: 构建Curation-Bench基准，固定模型、训练方案和评估套件，赋予智能体命令行权限以检查数据、实现策略、提交训练/评估流水线并迭代修改。在视觉-语言指令微调场景下测试现成智能体，并设计需要引用、实例化和改编已有方法的脚手架来引导方法探索。

Result: 现成智能体十次迭代内达到已发表的数据选择基线水平，但主要调优局部策略变体而非探索新策略家族。脚手架引导的智能体自主组合出超越强基线的方法，仅用其十分之一的数据预算。

Conclusion: 当前智能体能运行策展循环，但可靠的数据研究需要脚手架引导的方法适应，而非仅靠开放提示。

Abstract: Curating training data is among the most consequential yet labor-intensive parts of modern AI development: practitioners iteratively propose, implement, evaluate, and revise data policies against noisy benchmark feedback. We ask whether generalist coding agents can automate this data-curation loop. We introduce *Curation-Bench*, an agent-centric benchmark that fixes the model, training recipe, and evaluation suite while giving agents command-line access to inspect data, implement policies, submit them to a fixed training/evaluation pipeline, and revise. In a vision-language instruction-tuning instantiation, out-of-the-box agents reach strong published data-selection baselines within ten iterations. However, trajectory analysis reveals a persistent *execution-research gap*: agents mainly tune local policy variants rather than explore new policy families, even when given strategy guides and paper references. Scaffolds requiring each iteration to cite, instantiate, and adapt a prior method shift agents toward method-guided exploration. The scaffolded agent autonomously composes -- without human design input -- a data-selection policy that outperforms strong published baselines at one-tenth their data budget. Overall, current agents can run the curation loop, but reliable data research requires scaffolded method adaptation, not open-ended prompting alone. Code and benchmark are open-sourced.

</details>


### [108] [Characterizing initial human-AI proof formalization workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04273)
*Katherine M. Collins,Simon Frieder,Jonas Bayer,Jacob Loader,Jeck Lim,Peiyang Song,Fabian Zaiser,Lexin Zhou,Shanda Li,Sam Looi,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Umang Bhatt,Adrian Weller,Jose Hernandez-Orallo,Cameron E. Freer,Valerie Chen,Ilia Sucholutsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法分析，研究了AI工具在数学证明形式化工作流中的早期影响，发现尽管存在工具局限性，但使用AI辅助能提高形式化准确性，且用户偏好多样，普遍希望在保持人类高层控制的同时获得AI帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，自动验证数学证明的有效性一直是挑战。随着AI在代码生成和数学推理方面的进步，形式化证明的能力有望得到提升。现有研究多关注基准测试，本文则聚焦于人们如何使用这些AI工具及其对形式化工作流的影响。

Method: 采用混合方法分析：1) 定性问卷调查，了解用户对AI辅助形式化的偏好和障碍；2) 受控用户研究，让参与者在有无AI辅助下形式化不同难度和领域的数学问题及证明，观察实际使用模式。

Result: 用户偏好多样的，普遍期望AI辅助但保持人类对证明发现过程的高层控制。在受控实验中，尽管AI工具在自动形式化方面存在局限，使用AI的参与者的形式化准确率仍高于未使用者，且多数参与者灵活选择使用多种AI工具。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了AI融入形式化工作流的早期阶段，展示了人类与AI之间密切互动的协作模式，为理解AI如何在实际数学形式化中发挥作用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: For centuries, human mathematicians have written proofs to substantiate their mathematical arguments; yet, the ability to automatically verify the validity of proofs has long been a challenge. Advances in AI systems' ability to generate code and engage in increasingly high-level mathematical reasoning promise to transform people's ability to formalize and thereby verify proofs. While many works focus on benchmarking the current frontier, we instead study how people use these tools. We conduct a mixed-methods analysis into the initial impact of AI on people's formalization workflows: what people claim they want, what they see as the barriers to those visions, and how they actually use and adapt AI in practice. A qualitative survey shows that people's preferences are diverse, but with a general desire for AI assistance in formalization that preserves high-level human control over the proof discovery process. To assess how people actually engage with AI for formalization under such limitations, we conduct a controlled user study in which participants formalize informal math problems and their proofs, with and without AI, across a range of mathematical problems at varying levels of difficulty and domains. Despite limitations of the tools at the time for autoformalization, participants tend to attain higher formalization accuracy when allowed access to AI tools than when formalizing on their own, with most participants flexibly choosing to use multiple different AI tools. Taken together, our work sheds light on the early stages of AI integration into formalization workflows, involving an intimate interplay of human and AI engagement.

</details>


### [109] [The Saturation Trap and the Subjectivity of Intervention Timing: Why Affect-Based Triggers and LLM Judges Fail to Time Interventions on Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04296)
*Manvendra Modgil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主AI代理在长周期软件执行中的运行时安全干预时机问题。通过HEART情感动态引擎和SWE-bench-Verified调试轨迹，发现状态饱和陷阱、LLM判断器的能力门槛，以及人类标注者之间极低的干预判断一致性（Krippendorff's alpha仅+0.047），得出结论：干预时机是一个低信度的建构，不适宜以单一标注者的F1作为优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理从对话系统转向长周期软件执行时，运行时安全层（决定何时中断代理）变得至关重要。现有研究对干预时机的系统理解不足，缺乏对多种检测架构、人类标注一致性以及模型能力的联合评估。

Method: 使用18维情感动力学引擎（HEART）作为诊断探针，在SWE-bench-Verified调试轨迹上评估四类干预触发器（绝对状态阈值、复合状态-动作模式、正则推理特征提取、零样本LLM作为裁判），并与人工标注的干预点进行对比。

Result: 1) 状态饱和陷阱：代理在持续困难下无恢复信号，阈值触发器在39-83%的动作上持续触发；2) LLM判断器存在能力和上下文门槛：小模型从不触发，前沿模型仅在完整轨迹上下文中达到F1 0.17-0.40，成本高达90倍；3) 人工标注信度极低：三位训练标注者在56动作轨迹上，干预位置Krippendorff's alpha仅+0.047，干预类型一致性更低。

Conclusion: 干预时机是一个低信度的建构，单一标注者的F1不适合作为优化目标。本文的贡献在于联合绘制了人类标注者间信度、四种检测器架构、跨模型LLM裁判扫描以及复现的饱和效应，而非单一检测器的准确率。

Abstract: As autonomous AI agents move from conversational systems to long-horizon software execution, runtime safety layers that decide when to interrupt an agent have become essential. We study this timing problem using a continuous 18-dimensional affective-dynamics engine (HEART) as a diagnostic probe, evaluating four intervention trigger families - absolute state thresholds, composite state-action patterns, regex reasoning-feature extraction, and zero-shot LLM-as-judge - against human-annotated intervention points on SWE-bench-Verified debugging traces. We report three findings. First, a State Saturation Trap: agents show no recovery signal under sustained difficulty, so modeled frustration quickly crosses the threshold and stays at its maximum, converting threshold-on-state triggers from moment detectors into near-constant indicators that fire on 39-83% of actions across five trajectories. Second, a capability-and-context floor for LLM judges: a small model (gpt-5.4-mini) never fires, while frontier and cross-vendor models escape the zero-firing floor only with full-trajectory context, and even then reach only F1 0.17-0.40 at up to 90x the cost. Third, and most importantly, the supervised target is not reproducible among humans: three trained annotators using one rubric on a 56-action trajectory agree on where to intervene only slightly above chance (location Krippendorff's alpha = +0.047; best pairwise Cohen's kappa = +0.349) and not at all on intervention type (pause degenerate; clarify below chance; reflect only alpha = +0.226). We conclude that intervention timing is a low-reliability construct, making single-annotator F1 an unsuitable optimization target. Our contribution is the joint mapping of this problem across human inter-rater reliability, four detector architectures, a cross-model LLM-judge sweep, and a reproduced saturation effect, rather than any single detector's accuracy.

</details>


### [110] [Exploring Cross-Scenario Generality of Agentic Memory Systems: Diagnostics and a Strong Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04315)
*Zhikai Chen,Jialiang Gu,Junyu Yin,Xianxuan Long,Shenglai Zeng,Xiaoze Liu,Kai Guo,Keren Zhou,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文回顾了八种记忆系统及一个智能体框架，发现赋予智能体主动控制存储与检索的能力比被动存储更有效，并基于此提出了AutoMEM系统，在跨场景任务中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统大多针对单一场景（如多轮对话或单一路径）设计，缺乏在异构轨迹（多种复杂应用场景）下的泛化能力验证。

Method: 在五个不同场景（单轮问答、多会话聊天、智能体轨迹问答、记忆压力测试、长周期智能体任务）上，对八种记忆系统及一个智能体框架进行系统性评估。该框架通过工具调用自主管理纯文本文件存储，实现主动控制。

Result: 自主管理存储检索的智能体框架在跨任务排名中表现最佳，而被动管道式存储方法泛化能力差。

Conclusion: 记忆系统性能的关键在于赋予智能体对存储和检索的主动控制权，而非依赖固定的被动存储管道。基于此提出的AutoMEM系统在跨场景泛化性上优于所有评估系统。

Abstract: LLM agents accumulate histories that outgrow their context windows, motivating a growing literature on memory systems. Yet most existing designs are tuned to a single scenario (multi-session chat or a single trajectory format), and there is little evidence that they generalize across the heterogeneous trajectories agents encounter in deployment. We revisit eight memory systems plus an agentic harness for search problems, on five scenarios: single-turn QA, multi-session chat, agentic-trajectory QA, memory stress tests, and long-horizon agentic tasks. The harness, which self-manages flat text-file storage via tool calls, achieves the best cross-task ranking, suggesting that memory performance hinges on giving the agent active control over storage and retrieval rather than on a passive store behind a fixed pipeline. We instantiate this insight in AutoMEM, an agentic memory harness with a self-managed tool interface that achieves the best cross-scenario generality among the systems we evaluate.

</details>


### [111] [The Digital Apprentice: A Framework for Human-Directed Agentic AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04321)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"数字学徒"框架，通过方法论捕获、授权控制和持续对齐三大组件，让AI代理的自主权基于实证证据逐步提升，而非默认授予，从而实现可扩展且可信赖的智能代理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理部署面临两难困境：过多人类监督限制规模扩展，而过度自主权又导致责任失控，缺乏负责任的委托治理基础设施。

Method: 提出Digital Apprentice框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)方法论捕获——将人类专家的隐性方法提炼为结构化资产；(2)授权控制——自主权升级需经明确的人类批准；(3)持续对齐——运行时纠正漂移并将纠正转化为个性化偏好数据。

Result: 将该框架实例化为推理时控制平面，构建质量模型并讨论提升策略。在开放专业语料库上应用，证明在流量偏移下检测数据漂移并切换技术可恢复降级的质量维度。

Conclusion: 方法论捕获、授权控制和持续对齐三大支柱作为系统整合，为构建可扩展且不牺牲信任的代理系统提供了更安全可行的路径。

Abstract: Agentic AI deployments face a recurring design tension: heavy human oversight limits scale, while broad autonomy outruns accountability. Neither posture provides the governance infrastructure required for responsible delegation. We present the Digital Apprentice, a framework for scalable, safe AI agency in which autonomy is earned, not assumed. The Digital Apprentice is a developmental learner that internalizes the tacit methodology of a directing human, graduating through per-skill autonomy tiers only when empirical evidence justifies it. The result is an agent that becomes genuinely useful over time while remaining aligned to a specific human's standards. Three architectural components make this possible. (1) Methodology capture, distilling a directing professional's tacit approach into structured assets. (2) Authorization, with autonomy escalation gated by explicit human approval. (3) Continuous alignment, correcting drift at runtime and converting each correction into owned preference data. We instantiate this framework as an inference-time control plane.
